Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the polymers space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 827 and the convergence rate is O(11/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the polymers decomposition. By minimality, the element a^827 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 11-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
